  112 | function normalizeCheckerStats(value: any): CheckerStats {
  113 |   const legacyChecks = Number(value?.checks || 0);
  114 |   const base = emptyCheckerStats();
  115 |   const next = {
  116 |     LINK: normalizeEngineStats(value?.LINK),
  117 |     IMAGE: normalizeEngineStats(value?.IMAGE),
  118 |     VIDEO: normalizeEngineStats(value?.VIDEO),
  119 |     ULTIMATE: normalizeEngineStats(value?.ULTIMATE),
  120 |   };
  121 |   if (legacyChecks && !Object.values(next).some(item => item.runs || item.checked || item.defects)) {
  122 |     next.LINK.checked = legacyChecks;
  123 |   }
  124 |   return next;
  125 | }

MATCH @ line 140:

  138 | }
  139 | 
  140 | export function recordCheckerStats(
  141 |   userId: string,
  142 |   category: "LINK" | "IMAGE" | "VIDEO" | "ULTIMATE",
  143 |   outcome: "success" | "failed",
  144 |   summary?: StatSummary
  145 | ) {
  146 |   const store = readStore();
  147 |   const user = store.users.find(item => item.id === userId);
  148 |   if (!user) throw new Error("ACCOUNT NOT FOUND.");
  149 | 
  150 |   const stat = user.checkerStats[category];
  151 |   stat.runs += 1;
  152 |   if (outcome === "success") {
  153 |     stat.successful += 1;
  154 |     if (summary) {
  155 |       stat.checked += Number(summary.checked || 0);
  156 |       stat.manual += Number(summary.manual || 0);
  157 |       stat.defects += Number(summary.defects || 0);
  158 |     }
  159 |   } else {
  160 |     stat.failed += 1;
  161 |   }
  162 | 
  163 |   writeStore(store);
  164 |   return user;
  165 | }

  140 | export function recordCheckerStats(
  141 |   userId: string,
  142 |   category: "LINK" | "IMAGE" | "VIDEO" | "ULTIMATE",
  143 |   outcome: "success" | "failed",
  144 |   summary?: StatSummary
  145 | ) {
  146 |   const store = readStore();
  147 |   const user = store.users.find(item => item.id === userId);
  148 |   if (!user) throw new Error("ACCOUNT NOT FOUND.");
  149 | 
  150 |   const stat = user.checkerStats[category];
  151 |   stat.runs += 1;
  152 |   if (outcome === "success") {
  153 |     stat.successful += 1;
  154 |     if (summary) {
  155 |       stat.checked += Number(summary.checked || 0);
  156 |       stat.manual += Number(summary.manual || 0);
  157 |       stat.defects += Number(summary.defects || 0);
  158 |     }
  159 |   } else {
  160 |     stat.failed += 1;
  161 |   }
  162 | 
  163 |   writeStore(store);
  164 |   return user;
  165 | }

   16 | export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
   17 |   const user = getUserBySession(request.cookies.get(COOKIE)?.value);
   18 |   if (!user) {
   19 |     return NextResponse.json({ error: "AUTHENTICATION REQUIRED." }, { status: 401, headers: { "cache-control": "no-store" } });
   20 |   }
   21 | 
   22 |   const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
   23 |   const type = String(body?.type || "").toUpperCase() as CheckerType;
   24 |   if (!TYPES.includes(type)) {
   25 |     return NextResponse.json({ error: "INVALID CHECKER TYPE." }, { status: 400, headers: { "cache-control": "no-store" } });
   26 |   }
   27 | 
   28 |   try {
   29 |     const updated = recordCheckerStats(user.id, type, {
   30 |       runs: 1,
   31 |       checked: number(body?.checked),
   32 |       manual: number(body?.manual),
   33 |       defects: number(body?.defects),
   34 |     });
   35 |     return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, user: updated, checkerStats: updated.checkerStats }, { headers: { "cache-control": "no-store" } });

    6 | 
    7 | const COOKIE = "v1124_auth_session";
    8 | const TYPES = ["LINK", "IMAGE", "VIDEO", "ULTIMATE"] as const;
    9 | type CheckerType = typeof TYPES[number];
   10 | 
   11 | function number(value: unknown) {
   12 |   const n = Number(value);
   13 |   return Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0 ? Math.floor(n) : 0;
   14 | }
   15 | 
   16 | export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
   17 |   const user = getUserBySession(request.cookies.get(COOKIE)?.value);
   18 |   if (!user) {
   19 |     return NextResponse.json({ error: "AUTHENTICATION REQUIRED." }, { status: 401, headers: { "cache-control": "no-store" } });
   20 |   }
   21 | 
   22 |   const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
   23 |   const type = String(body?.type || "").toUpperCase() as CheckerType;
   24 |   if (!TYPES.includes(type)) {
   25 |     return NextResponse.json({ error: "INVALID CHECKER TYPE." }, { status: 400, headers: { "cache-control": "no-store" } });
   26 |   }
   27 | 
   28 |   try {
   29 |     const updated = recordCheckerStats(user.id, type, {
   30 |       runs: 1,
   31 |       checked: number(body?.checked),
   32 |       manual: number(body?.manual),
   33 |       defects: number(body?.defects),
   34 |     });
   35 |     return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, user: updated, checkerStats: updated.checkerStats }, { headers: { "cache-control": "no-store" } });
   36 |   } catch (error) {
   37 |     return NextResponse.json({ error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "CHECKER STATISTICS COULD NOT BE SAVED." }, { status: 400, headers: { "cache-control": "no-store" } });
   38 |   }
   39 | }

DETECTED CHECKER ENGINE KEYS
====================================================================================================
CheckerStats Record: "LINK" | "IMAGE" | "VIDEO" | "ULTIMATE", EngineStats>

emptyCheckerStats():

function emptyCheckerStats(): CheckerStats {
  return {
    LINK: emptyEngineStats(),
    IMAGE: emptyEngineStats(),
    VIDEO: emptyEngineStats(),
    ULTIMATE: emptyEngineStats(),
  }
